## Reale Honor-Menünavigation nach dem Instrumentierungslauf — 2026-10-19

## Phase-6 Responsive-Nachprüfung: Projektaktionen bei 400 dp/150 % — 2026-10-19

- Physische visuelle Honor-Prüfung mit temporär auf 400 dp simulierter Breite und 150 % Schrift deckte abgeschnittene Beschriftungen in den zwei Dateimenü-Kacheln auf („Modell la…“, „3MF sich…“). Bei 360 dp/130 % waren beide Labels lesbar; die 320-dp/150-%-Korrektur blieb ebenfalls lesbar.
- Ursache: Die adaptive Policy verwendete bei 400 dp/150 % noch zwei Spalten. Sie verwendet bei Schriftfaktor mindestens 1,5 bis einschließlich 400 dp nun eine breite Spalte mit zwei Reihen. Die Tests prüfen jetzt den Randwert 400 dp (eine Spalte) und 401 dp (zwei Spalten).
- `:app:testDebugUnitTest` und `:app:testSliceTestUnitTest` jeweils 562/562 bestanden; `BedMenuActionTileWidthPolicyTest` 9/9. Gezielter Honor-Test `InitialProjectMenuDeviceTest#initialProjectRouteShowsFileActionsOnItsFirstOpen` 1/1 bestanden.
- Die geöffnete Projektoberfläche nach der Änderung wurde auf Honor bei 400 dp/150 % visuell geprüft: beide Aktionen vollständig lesbar, alle sechs Workspace-Ziele sichtbar, Druckplatte weiter zentral. Bereits bei 360 dp/130 % und 320 dp/150 % geprüfte Darstellungen bleiben dokumentiert. Display-Override/Font-Scale danach auf 1280×2800, 560 dpi und 1,0 zurückgesetzt.
- Commitgebundener Debug-Build für Code `49412024b37c32135210aab1f2d999262ff03aa1`: `OrkanSlicer_49412024b3.apk`, 157,232,876 Bytes, SHA-256 `998A74D82A402456B96AEAFB9405F6998B5FDC2E411786D91D281F8D8056CB3F`. Auf Honor installiert/gestartet; Paket `com.codemastercody3d.orcaslicermobile`, Version 0.8.316 / Code 351. Letzte 5.000 Logcat-Zeilen ohne Crash-, ANR-, OOM- oder Fatal-Signal-Treffer.
- Phase 5 (vollständige Aktion-für-Aktion-Abnahme) und Phase 6 (manuelle TalkBack-Traversierung sowie alle physischen Größen/Ausrichtungen) sind nicht abgeschlossen. Die vollständige Mehrplatten-Projektpersistenz bleibt getrennt offen.

Keine Verbindung zu einem Drucker.
- Vollständige JVM-Suites: Debug 559/559 und Slice 559/559 bestanden, je 0 Fehler/Errors/Skips. `git diff --check` erfolgreich. Emulator `emulator-5554` bleibt offline.

Die echte OrkanSlicer-Oberfläche wurde bei 320 dp/150 % Schrift und ca. 400 dp/130 % Schrift visuell geprüft; danach wurden physische Displaygröße, Dichte, Schriftfaktor und Rotation wiederhergestellt. Beide Screenshots zeigen erreichbare Hauptnavigation und die zentrale Druckplatte. Logcat-Nachprüfung: kein Crash, ANR oder OOM im abgefragten Ausschnitt.
- Release-APK aus Code-Commit `4dfc9c640697e0bdcfe895d4bf58d7b71ec90c5e`: `app/build/outputs/apk/debug/OrkanSlicer_4dfc9c6406.apk`, 157,232,822 Bytes, SHA-256 `44B57226142B82546E922B9405E1DB7EA8631D4BF0517AB94DE11177C4C1CF44`. - Phase 4 is complete at the workspace architecture acceptance scope. `WorkspaceShell` owns the visible navigation, panel placement, and Compose surfaces; `RendererAdapter` keeps the same GLView in the Compose `AndroidView`; `BedFragment.currentMenuSlot` delegates to `WorkspaceController.selectedDestinationSlot` rather than keeping a second value; workspace overlays/tools have one controller/coordinator. Production-source search found no `UnfoldMenu` references or remaining View menu hosts; each route is required to implement `WorkspaceComposeBedMenu`. `BedFragment` retains Android lifecycle, GL/native/model, activity-result, and technical WebView integration only. Route transition/GL lifecycle, Back/cancel, paint cancellation, and panel bounds tests passed in the current Honor suite. Necessary fallback Android dialogs outside an attached workspace remain by design.
- Phase 5 is in progress. A source-based inventory found the six main route action counts: Project/Files 2; Model 13; Camera 9; Edit 17; Tools 3 plus optional AI generator; Slice 3 before slicing and 5 after slicing plus up to 2 configured-printer actions. These are action definitions, not all unique actions currently available in every project state.
- New menu-action instrumentation: the camera route test clicks all seven preset Compose tiles and verifies the renderer reaches each geometry target. `modelMenuDuplicateActionAppliesRequestedCopyCountToNativeModel` loads a disposable STL, enters two copies in the actual Compose dialog, confirms, and verifies the native model grows from one to three objects. `modelObjectsMenuAssignmentOverridesImportedThreeMfMaterial` loads the bundled two-color 3MF, drives Model → Objects → Assign → T2 and verifies object 0 changes to only T3 while object 1 remains T2 and old per-volume paint is absent. The full `InitialProjectMenuDeviceTest` class passed 6/6 on Honor; existing class coverage also verifies initial Project actions, profile-export dismissal, camera projection/control preferences, and all camera presets.

# Previous continuation — collapsed-route transition cleanup (2026-10-18, 0.8.301)

- Fixed a route guard ordering bug: `BedFragment` had compared the requested slot after `WorkspaceController.navigateToDestination()` updated it, marking transitions settled too early. Transition state is owned by `WorkspaceController`, and the prior destination is captured before navigation. A rapid second tap is rejected while the animated transition is active.
- Fixed a separate stuck-guard path for programmatic route changes while the compact portrait tool panel is collapsed (for example, after loading G-code). `WorkspaceShell` now settles route state at the shell level; collapsed portrait layout immediately clears the guard because no animated menu host exists, while visible menu hosts retain the animation delay.
- Added controller state coverage and extended Honor `WorkspaceNavigationDeviceTest` for rapid second taps and direct route changes while the portrait panel is collapsed. The first collapsed-route device attempt found a test reflection-signature mismatch; after using the actual two-parameter JVM method signature, the focused Honor test passed 1/1. Debug and Slice JVM suites passed 555/555 each, with 0 failures/errors/skips.

### Phase 4.1 — workspace dialog route audit (source call paths, 2026-10-17)

| Route / source | Active Workspace host and behavior | Remaining legacy path |
|---|---|---|
| `BedFragment.showWorkspaceMessageDialog` | `WorkspaceMessageDialog` on the exclusive `MESSAGE_DIALOG` panel; confirm and dismiss callbacks are resolved once; Back dismisses. Callers include model deletion, import errors, sync failures and slicing failures. | None while the Bed workspace host is available. |
| `BedFragment.showWorkspaceComposeDialog` | Common Compose host for tool/profile/config dialogs; clears the active tool before opening and uses the controller's exclusive overlay/back callback. | None while the Bed workspace host is available. |
| `MainActivity.show3mfImportChoice` | `ThreeMfImportChoiceDialog`; printer/print/filament controls start unchecked, apply updates temporary project settings, cancel leaves them cleared. | Manual Android `LinearLayout`/`Spinner`/`CheckBox` Material dialog only when no workspace host can be shown. |
| MainActivity profile import flow | `ProfileConfigImportDialog` handles warnings, profile selection, conflicts, overwrite/skip and cancel. | Sequential Android multi-choice/warning dialogs only in the no-workspace fallback branch. |
| `MainActivity.showFileOperationError`, `showSvgSimplifyPrompt`, `handleAppBack` | Workspace message/consent Compose surfaces; simplify runs only after explicit confirmation. | Material dialog fallback only outside an attached Bed workspace. |
| Image conversion routes in `ImageToStlConverter` / `ConverterProgressDialog` | Compose dialog content hosted by the workspace overlay when a Bed host is available. | Builder dialogs retained for invocations without a Bed workspace; Android document/image pickers remain platform integrations. |
| `BedFragment` slicing and upload progress | `SliceProgressDialog.Content` and `PrinterUploadProgressDialog.Content` are Compose surfaces mounted through `WorkspaceShell`/controller; these classes are not legacy View dialog hosts. | None in the active workspace path. |

Inventory result: across the audited MainActivity/BedFragment workspace paths, **0 active workspace-owned `BeamAlertDialogBuilder` or manual Android-View dialog routes** were found; the remaining builders above are conditional non-workspace fallbacks. Existing Compose import-choice tests cover independent print/filament selection; the .299 device suite covers dialog cancellation and key workspace routes. This is a source-path audit, not an exhaustive run of every possible dialog result callback. `CloudController` contains a separate Material conflict dialog in its snackbar callback, but its current `OFFLINE_BOOTSTRAP = true` guards prevent the cloud sync route from being entered; it is not hosted by BedFragment/WorkspaceController and remains outside the Phase 4 workspace-dialog count.

BedFragment remains the required technical host for the root `FrameLayout`, stable GL/RendererAdapter, and embedded printer `WebView`. Workspace destination and active tool/panel state now read from WorkspaceController; `menuToolsExpanded` and `currentMenuSlot` are delegated properties, not duplicate state. Orientation/font-scale measurement, ComposeView creation, renderer lifecycle, GL-thread operations, and WebView lifecycle remain technical bridge duties. Phase 4.3 is advanced but still needs a broader route/lifecycle regression pass.

- Updated the workspace navigation device regression to assert that the selected route view is attached and the prior route view is detached after transition.
- Added Honor Compose UI tests for destination accessibility/selection and large-text two-row navigation.

Their existing `onCreate`/`onDestroy`, callbacks, renderer operations, and system activity-result flows are retained; the former `ThemeAwareComposeView` implementations remain compatibility fallbacks for non-workspace callers.

## Farbpanel: Basisfilament über die echte Workspace-Aktion — 2026-10-19

- Erweitert `editPaintActionsOpenTheirGlModesAndBackCancelsWithoutMeshMutation`: die Compose-Aktion „Farbmalen“ öffnet das GL-gebundene Paintpanel, dessen Basisfilament-T2-Kachel wird ausgewählt und die Sitzung mit „Fertig“ bestätigt. Der native Modellzustand muss danach exakt T2 ausweisen, ohne künstliche Facettenfarbe und ohne Änderung der Dreiecksanzahl; Back-Abbruchprüfungen aller vier Paint-Modi bleiben bestehen.
- Fokussierter Honor-Test bestanden 1/1; anschließend vollständige `InitialProjectMenuDeviceTest` 34/34, 0 Skips/Fehler, Honor PTP-N49 / Android 16, JUnit-Zeit `2026-10-19T10:39:33` UTC. `emulator-5554` offline.
- Eine experimentelle synthetische GL-Touchgeste änderte die native Facetten-Paint-Zuordnung nicht und wurde nicht als bestanden gewertet. Der abgeschlossene Test prüft die sichtbare Basisfilament-Aktion; die separate native `PaintSession`-Facettenpersistenz bleibt durch vorhandene Roundtriptests abgedeckt. Manueller GL-Pinselstrich über Gerätetouch bleibt offen.
- Nur Android-Instrumentierungstest und Dokumentation geändert; kein Produktionscode und kein neues Laufzeit-APK. Phase 4 bleibt abgeschlossen; Phase 5 und 6 bleiben offen. Nächster Schritt: weitere nicht abgedeckte sichere Workspace-/globale Handler aus der Aktionsmatrix prüfen.
